新冠与流感联合检测记录指南

英国发布新冠与流感联合快速检测记录保存指南

查看详细摘要

英国政府发布了关于使用组合快速横向流动设备(LFD)同时检测COVID-19和流感的记录保存指南。该指南提供了'组合快速LFD测试多重注册记录表'电子表格，用于记录每日使用组合快速LFD测试检测的人员详情。根据《健康保护(通知)条例2010》第4A条的规定，相关机构应使用该电子表格注册组合快速LFD测试结果。同时提供了'如何报告SARS-CoV-2和流感A+B抗原组合快速测试的个体测试结果'的完整说明文档。该指南已更新至最新版本。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 日期不详: 英国政府发布新冠与流感联合快速检测记录保存指南
  • 日期不详: 提供'组合快速LFD测试多重注册记录表'电子表格
  • 日期不详: 发布'如何报告SARS-CoV-2和流感A+B抗原组合快速测试的个体测试结果'文档
  • 日期不详: 更新至最新版本
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法，主要涉及使用组合快速横向流动设备(LFD)进行COVID-19和流感检测，以及相关结果的记录和报告方法。

🇬🇧 English summary

The UK government has issued guidance on recording details of individuals tested for COVID-19 and influenza using combo rapid lateral flow device (LFD) tests. The guidance provides a 'Record-keeping multiple registration spreadsheet for combo rapid LFD tests' Excel file for settings to register daily test results. According to Regulation 4A of the Health Protection (Notification) Regulations 2010, settings should use this spreadsheet to report COVID-19 and flu combo rapid LFD test results. Complete instructions are available in the document 'How to report individual test results for SARS-CoV-2 and influenza A+B antigen combo rapid tests'. The guidance has been updated to the latest version.

报道未提及具体治疗方法，仅提及疫苗接种作为预防RSV感染的措施

🇬🇧 English summary

The maternal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination program was introduced in England in 2024 as part of a wider immunization program to protect vulnerable populations and prevent RSV infection in infants. Official statistics provide monthly vaccination coverage reports from September 2025 to June 2026, including data downloads for each month. The program represents a significant public health measure by England's health authorities to control RSV transmission through targeted vaccination of pregnant women.

UKHSA · 英国卫生安全局🦠 水痘✨ AI 整理 2026-10-30 18:21

英国水痘疫情：超3000婴幼儿住院

英国2025年水痘致3000多名儿童住院，2026年起纳入NHS常规疫苗接种计划

查看详细摘要

英国卫生安全局(UKHSA)发布的《水痘年度报告2025》显示，2025年英格兰18岁以下儿童因水痘住院人数达3037人。监测显示，英格兰约三分之一的全科诊所记录显示，18岁以下儿童有超过15000例水痘就诊案例。5岁以下儿童受影响最严重，占所有临床报告的70%以上。不满1岁的儿童住院率最高，每10万人中有5.8例，而1-4岁儿童为2.3例。2026年1月1日起，水痘防护已纳入常规儿童免疫计划，作为麻疹、腮腺炎、风疹和水痘(MMRV)联合疫苗的一部分。英国卫生安全局呼吁符合条件的家长带孩子接种MMRV疫苗。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2025年：英国卫生安全局发布《水痘年度报告2025》，显示3037名18岁以下儿童因水痘住院
  • 2026年1月1日：水痘防护纳入常规儿童免疫计划，作为MMRV联合疫苗的一部分
  • 2026年11月1日至2028年3月31日：针对2020年1月至2022年8月出生儿童的补种计划
  • 日期不详：英国卫生安全局呼吁符合条件的家长带孩子接种MMRV疫苗
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

从2026年1月1日起，水痘防护已纳入常规儿童免疫计划，作为麻疹、腮腺炎、风疹和水痘(MMRV)联合疫苗的一部分。儿童接种2剂，第一剂在12个月大时，第二剂在18个月大时。针对2020年1月至2022年8月出生且未得过水痘或未接种过2剂水痘疫苗的儿童，提供单剂MMRV疫苗补种。英国卫生安全局建议家长带孩子接种，以预防水痘、麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹等严重疾病。

🇬🇧 English summary

The UK Health Security Agency's (UKHSA) Varicella Annual Report 2025 reveals the significant impact of chickenpox on young children in England before its inclusion in the routine NHS Childhood Vaccination Programme. In 2025, there were 3,037 hospitalizations for chickenpox among children and young people under 18. Monitoring of about one-third of GP practices showed over 15,000 GP consultations for chickenpox in under-18s. Children aged 5 and under accounted for over 70% of clinical reports, with the highest hospitalization rates among infants under one year old (5.8 per 100,000). From January 1, 2026, chickenpox protection has been added to the routine childhood immunization schedule as part of the MMRV vaccine. A catch-up program is also running from November 1, 2026, to March 31, 2028, for children born between January 2020 and August 2022. UKHSA urges parents to vaccinate their child

UKHSA · 英国卫生安全局🦠 脊髓灰质炎✨ AI 整理 2026-10-30 17:30

白喉破伤风脊髓灰质炎疫苗指南更新

英国发布白喉破伤风脊髓灰质炎疫苗新版使用指南

查看详细摘要

英国政府发布白喉、破伤风和灭活脊髓灰质炎疫苗(Td/IPV – Revaxis®)的患者组方向(PGD)模板更新。该模板支持国家免疫计划，为10岁以上人群接种此疫苗，也用于旅行或相关病例和接触者的管理。Td/IPV (Revaxis®) PGD版本7.0的有效期为2026年12月4日至2029年11月4日。使用此PGD模板前必须获得授权，并应参考当前国家指南和疫苗产品特性摘要使用。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 日期不详: 发布白喉、破伤风和灭活脊髓灰质炎疫苗患者组方向(PGD)模板
  • 日期不详: 添加版本04.00
  • 日期不详: 添加版本05.00
  • 日期不详: 添加版本06.00
  • 2026年12月4日: Td/IPV (Revaxis®) PGD版本7.0生效
  • 2029年11月4日: Td/IPV (Revaxis®) PGD版本7.0到期
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

该PGD模板指导白喉、破伤风和脊髓灰质炎疫苗(Td/IPV – Revaxis®)的接种，适用于10岁以上人群。疫苗用于预防相关疾病，也可用于病例和接触者的管理。使用前必须获得授权，并应参考国家指南和疫苗产品特性摘要。

🇬🇧 English summary

The UK government has updated the patient group direction (PGD) template for diphtheria, tetanus and inactivated poliomyelitis vaccine (Td/IPV – Revaxis®). This template supports the national immunization program for individuals aged 10 and above, as well as for travel or management of cases and contacts of these diseases. The Td/IPV (Revaxis®) PGD version 7.0 is valid from December 4, 2026, to November 4, 2029. Practitioners must obtain authorization before using this PGD template and should refer to current national guidance and vaccine product characteristics.

💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法。指南主要关注麻疹病例管理、风险评估、暴露后预防时间窗口定义以及信息传播，未涉及具体治疗药物或方法。

🇬🇧 English summary

UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) has updated the national measles guidelines to version 8, providing comprehensive guidance for healthcare professionals on managing suspected measles cases. The updated document includes new references to MMRV vaccine, clarifies responsibilities for risk assessment in healthcare settings, and adds an additional section defining the time window for receiving post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). The guidelines also feature translated versions of warn and inform letters and factsheets in multiple languages, along with easy-to-read versions for accessibility. These resources aim to standardize measles case management, improve communication with potentially exposed individuals, and enhance public understanding of measles prevention and control measures.

  • 2026年7月30日：发布第一季度报告初版

  • 2021-2025年：英国发布各年度麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹实验室确诊病例报告
  • 日期不详：早期报告由公共卫生英格兰(PHE)发布

报道未提及具体治疗方法。该报告主要提供麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹的实验室确诊病例数据，未涉及针对这些疾病的具体治疗药物、疫苗或防控措施。

🇬🇧 English summary

The UK Health Security Agency has published laboratory-confirmed case reports for measles, mumps and rubella for 2026. The Q2 (April-June) and Q1 (January-March) reports have been released, with the Q1 report updated to version 2. The mumps case numbers in the Q1 report were revised to account for changes in surveillance methods introduced in August 2026. These methodological changes were applied retrospectively to all 2026 data. The reports are confirmed by the UK Health Security Agency's Virus Reference Department or local laboratory testing. Earlier reports were published by Public Health England (PHE). Specific case numbers and mortality data are not provided in the current reports.

禽流感管理指南更新

英国更新禽流感暴露病例和零星人类病例管理指南

查看详细摘要

英国政府更新了禽流感管理指南，包括处理接触禽流感的人类个体以及零星人类禽流感病例及其接触者的指导。指南更新了针对感染禽流感A(H5)亚型鸟类或其他动物人群的管理建议，并新增了英格兰地区零星禽流感人类溢出病例及其接触者的管理指南。指南提供了抗病毒预防用药的剂量指导，建议在超出指导范围的情况下联系当地健康保护团队获取建议和支持。

🕐 事件时间线

  • 日期不详: 新增'Managing sporadic avian influenza human spillover cases and their contacts in England'指南

💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

🇬🇧 English summary

The UK government has updated guidance for managing avian influenza exposures and sporadic human cases. The updated documents include 'Guidance for the management of people exposed to birds or other animals infected with avian influenza' and 'Managing sporadic avian influenza human spillover cases and their contacts in England'. These superseded previous guidance documents. The guidance provides dosages for antiviral prophylaxis for avian influenza exposures involving any subtype. Local health protection teams should be contacted for advice and support, particularly in situations outside the scope of the guidance.

WHO/CDC/ECDC/PAHO · 官方疫情通报🦠 呼吸道合胞病毒病✨ AI 整理 2026-10-28 17:02

欧洲新生儿RSV疫苗接种可减少住院率

RSV疫苗接种可显著减少欧洲婴儿住院率

查看详细摘要

欧洲疾病预防控制中心(ECDC)表示，随着寒冷季节临近，对新生儿进行RSV(呼吸道合胞病毒)的广泛疫苗接种可能显著减少欧洲婴儿的住院率。这一建议基于RSV在寒冷季节对婴儿健康的威胁，以及疫苗接种可能带来的公共卫生效益。

💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC) reports that as the cold season approaches, universal immunization of newborns against RSV (Respiratory Syncytial Virus) could significantly reduce infant hospitalizations across Europe. 🇬🇧 English summary

The UK government has updated its guidance on the use of antiviral agents for the treatment and prophylaxis of seasonal influenza. Major updates include: changing the working definition for severity of influenza disease from uncomplicated/complicated to non-severe/severe; updating information on influenza antiviral resistance; adding description and advice on the new antiviral baloxavir marboxil; updating treatment recommendation tables; and changing recommendations for prescribing influenza antivirals out-of-season in primary care. The guidance also incorporates new evidence on treating critically ill patients with oseltamivir and updated recommendations on renal dosing. Previously published by Public Health England.

🇬🇧 English summary

The ObLong study evaluated the impact of asymptomatic COVID-19 testing on NHS acute hospitals from 2022 to 2023. The research aimed to assess the effectiveness of routine asymptomatic testing of healthcare workers in reducing transmission, cost-effectiveness, and mitigating COVID-19's overall impact. The evaluation was conducted within varying local interpretations of patient testing policies. The study also identified facilitators and barriers encountered by NHS trusts when implementing different testing approaches. The comprehensive report spans 96 pages and provides insights into the challenges and benefits of asymptomatic testing strategies in healthcare settings during the pandemic.

欧洲小反刍兽疫疫情蔓延

小反刍兽疫2024年7月起在欧洲东南部多国爆发，已致183起疫情

查看详细摘要

小反刍兽疫(PPR)自2024年7月以来在欧洲东南部地区蔓延。病毒序列分析和实地调查显示，罗马尼亚东部是病毒传入和区域传播的源头。截至2026年1月，希腊、罗马尼亚、保加利亚、匈牙利、阿尔巴尼亚、科索沃和克罗地亚等7个国家共报告183起疫情，导致数十万只羊和山羊被大规模扑杀。疫情主要通过合法活体动物贸易传播，非法贸易和间接传播也起到一定作用。欧洲地区的临床症状变异较大，死亡率较低(0%-10%)，且症状不明显，容易被误诊或忽视，导致疾病难以早期发现和控制。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2018年：保加利亚出现小反刍兽疫有限传入
  • 2024年7月：小反刍兽疫在欧洲东南部重新出现
  • 2024年7月-2026年1月：7个国家报告183起疫情
  • 2026年：欧洲经历有记录以来最大规模的小反刍兽疫流行
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

欧盟将PPR列为A类动物疾病，要求在受感染场所立即实施根除措施，包括移动限制、加强监测和扑杀感染畜群。然而，由于临床症状变异大且不明显，早期检测和防控面临挑战。

🇬🇧 English summary

Peste des petits ruminants (PPR) disease has spread in southeastern Europe since July 2024. Virus sequence analyses indicate that eastern Romania was the point of introduction and source of regional dissemination. From July 2024 to January 2026, a total of 183 outbreaks were officially reported across seven countries (Greece, Romania, Bulgaria, Hungary, Albania, Kosovo, and Croatia), resulting in the mass culling of hundreds of thousands of small ruminants. Cross-border transmission was strongly associated with official records of live animal trade, with additional roles for illegal trade and indirect transmission. The clinical picture in Europe is highly variable, with many outbreaks associated with lower mortality rates (0%-10%) and few or no clinical signs, making detection challenging despite control measures including movement restrictions, enhanced surveillance, and culling of infe

查看详细摘要

法国研究人员对8个城市褐鼠进行采样，发现209/686只老鼠(30.5%)肝脏中存在鼠源戊型肝炎病毒(rHEV)RNA，所有8个城市均发现阳性老鼠，阳性率从9.7%到44%不等。体重>100g的老鼠阳性率(40.7%)显著高于<100g的老鼠(9%)。317份样本中144份(45%)检测到rHEV抗体。研究获得20个全基因组序列，发现法国和欧洲的鼠株与人类序列具有高度遗传相似性。rHEV似乎在法国城市鼠群中呈地方性流行。戊型肝炎病毒可导致人类急性或慢性肝炎，2018年首次在香港报道人类感染rHEV病例，随后在亚洲、北美和欧洲也有报道。法国已报告2例人类rHEV感染病例，但未记录与老鼠的直接接触。老鼠是rHEV的主要宿主，直接或间接接触可能构成暴露风险。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2010年：德国首次在褐鼠中发现Rocahepevirus ratti (rHEV)
  • 2010-2012年：法国里昂研究报道褐鼠中rHEV基因组阳性率为14%
  • 2018年：香港首次报道人类感染rHEV导致持续性肝炎
  • 2021-2023年：法国8城市开展褐鼠rHEV调查研究
  • 日期不详：法国报告首例人类rHEV感染病例
  • 日期不详：法国报告第二例人类rHEV感染病例
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

French researchers sampled brown rats in 8 cities and detected rat hepatitis E virus (rHEV) RNA in 209/686 (30.5%) rats, with positive animals found in all cities. Positivity rates ranged from 9.7% to 44%, with higher rates in rats >100g (40.7%) compared to <100g (9%). Antibodies were detected in 45% of samples. Twenty full genome sequences were obtained, showing high genetic identity between rat and human strains in France and Europe. rHEV appears to be enzootic in urban rat populations. HEV causes acute or chronic hepatitis in humans, with the first human rHEV case reported in Hong Kong in 2018. Subsequent cases have been documented in Asia, North America, and Europe, including two cases in France without documented rat contact. Rats are the primary reservoir, and direct or indirect contact poses potential exposure risk.

埃及家禽流感病毒暴露研究

埃及研究发现家畜存在H5N1和H9N2禽流感病毒抗体

查看详细摘要

埃及北部开展血清学研究，检测444份家畜血清样本，发现H9N2抗体阳性率2.5%，H5N1抗体阳性率2.9%-4.5%。研究针对2024年10月至2026年1月期间收集的样本，来自3个省份的33个小型农场和后院。研究旨在确定禽流感病毒是否向家畜溢出，特别是在混合物种环境中。H5N1病毒此前已在美国奶牛中检测到，并在2026年在荷兰农场发现感染猫。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2024年：美国奶牛中发现H5N1病毒
  • 2024年10月-2026年1月：埃及北部收集血清样本
  • 2026年：荷兰农场发现猫感染H5N1病毒，牛有抗体
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

A serological study in northern Egypt detected avian influenza virus antibodies in livestock. Between October 2024 and January 2026, researchers collected 444 serum samples from cattle in small farms and backyards across three governorates. Results showed 2.5% of tested animals had H9N2 antibodies and 2.9%-4.5% had H5N1 antibodies. The study aimed to determine if endemic avian influenza viruses are spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt, particularly in mixed-species settings. This research follows detection of H5N1 in US dairy cattle in 2024 and subsequent infections in other livestock species including goats, sheep, and alpacas in the US and UK.

The previous two deaths were caused by the H5N1 strain, while the Washington case resulted from the rarer H5N5 strain after cattle exposure. Scientists note that recent cases have been milder than expected, causing both relief and puzzlement. Meanwhile, New York state announced its first measles-related death in 30 years.

西班牙新冠后隐孢子虫病新亚型传播

西班牙新冠防控后隐孢子虫病高传染性新亚型IfA12G1R5迅速传播

查看详细摘要

隐孢子虫病是欧洲公共卫生问题，2020年欧盟报告发病率为1.7/10万人口，实际可能被低估。新冠疫情防控措施改变了西班牙隐孢子虫病的传播模式，高传染性人适应型gp60亚型如IfA12G1R5迅速出现并扩张，而主要人畜共患的C. parvum传播保持相对稳定。研究收集了2013-2019年疫情前和2023年疫情后的临床样本，由西班牙国家微生物学中心国家参考实验室进行基因分型分析。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2010年1月-2020年12月：PubMed等数据库搜索西班牙隐孢子虫分子研究
  • 2013年12月-2019年12月：收集疫情前隐孢子虫阳性临床样本
  • 2020年：新冠疫情防控措施实施，改变隐孢子虫病传播模式
  • 2023年3月-12月：收集疫情后隐孢子虫阳性临床样本
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

Cryptosporidiosis is a public health concern in Europe with reported incidence of 1.7 cases/100,000 population in the EU in 2020, likely underestimated due to underdiagnosis. COVID-19 containment measures in 2020 selectively altered Cryptosporidium transmission in Spain, leading to rapid emergence and expansion of hypertransmissible human-adapted gp60 subtypes like IfA12G1R5, while zoonotic C. parvum transmission remained stable. Researchers collected pre-pandemic samples from December 2013 to December 2019 and post-pandemic samples from March to December 2023 across multiple tertiary hospitals. All samples underwent confirmatory testing and genotyping at the gp60 marker at the Spanish National Centre for Microbiology's National Reference Laboratory.

加拿大新斯科舍省隐球菌感染病例

加拿大新斯科舍省2016-2024年报告6例隐球菌感染病例，5例为本地感染

查看详细摘要

加拿大新斯科舍省2016-2024年间确认6例新生隐球菌(Cryptococcus gattii)感染病例。其中5例位于该省西部，基因型为VGIIb，包括4例无省外旅行史，表明可能为本地感染。2014年，该省一只白尾鹿中发现VGIIb-like菌株，进一步支持当地获得性和区域流行性。新生隐球菌是一种环境真菌病原体，可引起严重的中枢神经系统和肺部疾病，包括脑膜脑炎和隐球菌瘤，常发生在无免疫抑制条件的人群中。该病原体在1999年温哥华岛爆发后在不列颠哥伦比亚省确立流行性，2008年和2015年魁北克分别报告了2例人类感染。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 1999年：不列颠哥伦比亚省温哥华岛爆发隐球菌感染
  • 2008年：魁北克报告2例人类感染病例
  • 2014年：新斯科舍省一只白尾鹿中发现VGIIb-like菌株
  • 2016-2024年：新斯科舍省确认6例新生隐球菌感染病例
  • 2015年：魁北克报告另1例人类感染病例
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

Nova Scotia, Canada reported 6 confirmed Cryptococcus gattii infections between 2016-2024. Five of these cases in western Nova Scotia were genotype VGIIb, including four cases without travel outside the province, suggesting possible local acquisition. In 2014, a VGIIb-like strain was identified in a white-tailed deer in Nova Scotia, supporting regional endemicity. C. gattii is an environmental fungal pathogen causing severe central nervous system and pulmonary diseases, often in immunocompetent individuals with an incubation period of 2-11 months. The pathogen first emerged in British Columbia after a 1999 Vancouver Island outbreak, with subsequent human infections reported in Quebec in 2008 and 2015.

STAT News · 卫生新闻🦠 麻疹✨ AI 整理 2026-10-30 16:30

麻疹历史信件揭示疫情现状

1834年麻疹信件与2026年美国麻疹疫情对比

查看详细摘要

本文通过分析1834年Jane Butt关于麻疹的信件，对比历史与当前疫情。1834年，Broughton村爆发麻疹，出现36例病例，部分患者出现严重肺部炎症，但全部康复，患者使用Morison's pills治疗。2026年美国麻疹疫情严重，已有超过4500例病例，是自1991年以来最多的，麻疹疫苗接种率降至92.4%，低于防止疫情传播所需水平。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 1834年9月23日：Jane Butt写信描述麻疹在Broughton村的传播情况
  • 1837年：Medical Dissenter期刊成为Morison's pills的官方伦敦分销点
  • 2026年：美国麻疹病例超过4500例，为1991年以来最多
  • 2025-2026学年：美国幼儿园疫苗接种豁免率达4.2%，麻疹疫苗接种率降至92.4%
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道中提及1834年麻疹患者使用了Morison's pills(一种泻药)进行治疗，但现代医学已证实麻疹没有特效治疗，主要依靠疫苗接种预防。当前麻疹疫苗接种率下降导致疫情蔓延。

🇬🇧 English summary

This article examines a 1834 letter from Jane Butt about a measles outbreak in Broughton village, comparing historical and current situations. In 1834, 36 measles cases were reported, with some developing severe lung inflammation, but all recovered after treatment with Morison's pills. In 2026, the U.S. faces its worst measles outbreak since 1991 with over 4,500 cases reported. Measles vaccination coverage has dropped to 92.4%, below the level needed to prevent outbreaks, with 4.2% of kindergarteners exempt from required vaccines.

巴西监测多种蚊媒病毒传播

巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州发现奥罗普切病毒等多种蚊媒病毒

查看详细摘要

研究人员在巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部地区进行了分子监测，研究奥罗普切病毒、马雅罗病毒、西尼罗河病毒和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的传播情况。研究收集了824名急性发热患者、3,484只蚊子和113只非人灵长类动物的样本。所有样本对登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒检测均为阴性，仅发现1例输入性奥罗普切病毒感染。2024年，该州因虫媒病毒活动增加宣布进入公共卫生紧急状态，并报告了首例奥罗普切病毒感染。血清学和分子证据表明西尼罗河病毒和圣路易斯脑炎病毒在该州的马匹中存在循环，尽管尚未确认人类圣路易斯脑炎病例。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2019年：开始收集蚊子样本

  • 2023年：开始收集人类样本
  • 2024年：米纳斯吉拉斯州因虫媒病毒活动增加宣布进入公共卫生紧急状态
  • 2024年：报告首例奥罗普切病毒感染
  • 2025年：研究持续进行中
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

Researchers conducted molecular surveillance for Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and Saint Louis encephalitis viruses in northern Minas Gerais, Brazil. The study tested samples from 824 patients with acute febrile illness, 3,484 mosquitoes, and 113 nonhuman primates. All samples were negative for dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika virus, with only one imported Oropouche virus infection detected. In 2024, the state declared a public health emergency due to increased arboviral activity, including the first reported OROV infection. Serologic and molecular evidence suggests circulation of WNV and SLEV in equids, though no confirmed human SLEV cases.

CDC《新发传染病》期刊 EID🦠 登革热✨ AI 整理 2026-10-27 12:00

古巴哈瓦那奥罗普切热疫情爆发

奥罗普切热在古巴哈瓦那爆发，549例疑似病例中189例确诊

查看详细摘要

奥罗普切热是由奥罗普切病毒引起的一种新兴虫媒病毒疾病，2024年在古巴全国爆发。哈瓦那地区报告549例疑似病例，其中189例(34.4%)确诊，无重症病例，发病率为8.9/10万，27-59岁成人发病率最高(10.2/10万)。疫情最初在圣地亚哥·德古巴和西恩富戈斯省被检测到，3个月内扩散至全国109个市镇，涉及所有15个省。截至年底，全国共报告24,259例疑似病例，626例确诊。古巴卫生部通过被动性虫媒病毒监测系统进行疫情监测，登革热共流行环境对监测系统提出挑战。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2024年5月27日：古巴卫生部报告奥罗普切热疫情
  • 2024年6月1日-9月7日：哈瓦那省发热监测数据分析期间
  • 2024年3个月内：疫情扩散至全国109个市镇
  • 2024年底：全国共报告24,259例疑似病例，626例确诊
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

报道未提及具体治疗方法

🇬🇧 English summary

Oropouche fever (OF), an emerging arboviral disease caused by Oropouche virus (OROV), emerged nationwide in Cuba in 2024. In Havana, 549 suspected cases were investigated with 189 (34.4%) confirmed. No severe cases were reported, with incidence at 8.9/100,000 population, highest in adults aged 27-59 years (10.2/100,000). Three infants tested positive. The outbreak was first detected in Santiago de Cuba and Cienfuegos Provinces in May 2024 and spread to 109 municipalities across all 15 provinces within 3 months. By year-end, Cuba reported 24,259 suspected cases and 626 confirmed cases nationwide. The outbreak challenged surveillance in a dengue-endemic setting.

🇬🇧 English summary

In October 2025, a 79-year-old man from Shandong Province, China, presented with fever and syncope and was diagnosed with myelodysplastic syndrome. Bone marrow examination showed hemophagocytosis and macrophages resembling typhoid cells, but cultures excluded Salmonella infection. Targeted next-generation sequencing identified Orientia tsutsugamushi, and a concealed popliteal eschar was found. This case demonstrates that scrub typhus can produce typhoid-like bone marrow morphology. Scrub typhus remains a major cause of acute undifferentiated fever across the Asia-Pacific region, with approximately 1 million cases reported annually. In China, the disease has undergone dramatic geographic expansion, with incidence increasing >20-fold during 2006-2023 and spreading from its historical focus in southern China to previously nonendemic northern provinces.

专家解析肺鼠疫相关知识

UPMC专家详解肺鼠疫症状、传播及治疗，称无需恐慌

查看详细摘要

UPMC传染病专家John Goldman博士就肺鼠疫相关知识进行了解释。肺鼠疫是由鼠疫耶尔森菌引起的感染类型之一，当细菌被吸入肺部时会导致快速进展的肺炎，未经治疗死亡率达100%。症状包括发烧、寒战、出汗、头痛、疲劳和肌肉酸痛，通常迅速发展为呼吸困难和败血症。肺鼠疫主要通过患者咳嗽或喷嚏产生的呼吸道飞沫传播，需要面对面近距离接触(3-6英尺)。在美国，肺鼠疫极为罕见，典型年份不会出现病例。诊断可通过血液、痰液或淋巴结液样本进行，治疗需在症状出现后24小时内静脉注射抗生素。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 日期不详: 俄罗斯政府与世界卫生组织就西伯利亚一名病原体实验室工作人员死亡是否与肺鼠疫有关发生争执
  • 日期不详: 美国全国范围内肺鼠疫谷歌搜索量激增
  • 日期不详: UPMC传染病专家John Goldman博士就肺鼠疫相关知识进行了解释
💊 治疗与防控（据报道整理，非医疗建议）

肺鼠疫需在症状出现后24小时内进行静脉注射抗生素治疗。通常使用氨基糖苷类抗生素(庆大霉素或链霉素)和氟喹诺酮类抗生素(环丙沙星或左氧氟沙星)联合治疗。由于病情严重，一般需要同时使用这两类抗生素各一种。诊断可通过血液、痰液或淋巴结液样本进行显微镜检查、细菌培养或聚合酶链反应检测。

🇬🇧 English summary

UPMC infectious disease specialist Dr. John Goldman explains pneumonic plague, a type of infection caused by Yersinia pestis bacteria. When inhaled into the lungs, it causes rapidly progressive pneumonia that is 100% fatal without treatment. Symptoms include fever, chills, headache, fatigue, and muscle aches, progressing quickly to shortness of breath and sepsis. Pneumonic plague spreads through respiratory droplets from coughing or sneezing, requiring close face-to-face contact (3-6 feet). It is much less infectious than COVID-19 or the flu. In the US, pneumonic plague is extremely rare with no typical cases reported annually. Diagnosis can be made from blood, sputum, or lymph fluid samples. Treatment requires intravenous antibiotics within 24 hours of symptom onset.

🇬🇧 English summary

A pneumonic plague outbreak has occurred in Russia's Irkutsk Anti-Plague Institute, where 28-year-old lab assistant Darya Shipilova died after being exposed to plague bacteria. Nearly 200 people who may have had contact with Shipilova have been quarantined. Russia's chief state sanitary doctor Anna Popova has traveled to Siberia to oversee the response, and a mask mandate has been implemented in Irkutsk. The US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention notes this plague variant can spread through respiratory droplets containing Yersinia pestis bacteria. Russian officials report no symptoms among potential contacts, though antibiotics are reportedly running low in the region.

俄罗斯西伯利亚伊尔库茨克抗鼠疫研究所发生鼠疫疫情，28岁实验室助理达里娅·希皮洛娃因打破装有活鼠疫细菌的试管而感染，入院两天后死亡。近200名可能与希皮洛娃有过接触的人员被隔离。俄罗斯首席国家卫生医生安娜·波波娃已从莫斯科飞往西伯利亚处理此事，伊尔库茨克已实施口罩令。俄罗斯官员称潜在接触者未出现症状，但报道称该地区抗生素已用尽。

Nearly 200 people who may have had contact with Shipilova have been quarantined. Russia's chief state sanitary doctor Anna Popova traveled from Moscow to Siberia to oversee the response, and a mask mandate has been implemented in Irkutsk. 【说法】发帖人连续追问：鼠疫由什么引起、源自何处、是否在实验室制造，暗示鼠疫起源可能并非自然，而是人为制造。【传播手法】以三个简短疑问句组成帖文，本身不作断言，但把“实验室制造”作为选项之一抛出，引导读者自行联想，疑问句式也便于转发扩散。【背景】鼠疫由 Yersinia pestis 细菌引起，是自然界存在的病原体，可经跳蚤叮咬、接触染疫动物及呼吸道飞沫传播；“实验室制造鼠疫”无依据、未证实。伊尔库茨克事件的实验室感染说法亦未获官方实验室结果确认。【时间线】2026-09-29 前后伊尔库茨克人员住院；2026-10-01 至 02 日死亡；2026-10-07 前后 CDC 与 WHO 表态监测；2026-10-31 帖文发布。

查看详细摘要

【說法】醫療機構發布衛教內容指出，人類可經跳蚤叮咬、接觸受感染動物或吸入感染性飛沫而感染鼠疫，感染後應立即接受抗生素治療，並提醒避免接觸野生齧齒動物、不處理生病動物、遠離可能攜帶跳蚤的齧齒動物。【傳播手法】以條列式衛教貼文發布，內容為通用防疫知識，不針對某一具體疫情通報，屬於預防宣傳而非事件調查。【背景】鼠疫確為細菌性疾病，抗生素治療有效，肺鼠疫未及時治療的死亡率極高，因此早期識別與及時用藥是各國衛生指引的共同要點；衛教內容本身與伊爾庫茨克事件的病因認定無關，不應視為對該事件的證實。【時間線】事件經過見時間線欄位。

【說法】@robinmonotti 稱安妮·雅各布森（Annie Jacobsen）2026年出版的《Biological War: A Scenario》具有預言性，暗示書中西伯利亞實驗室事故情節，與伊爾庫茨克研究所人員死亡、接觸者隔離的消息吻合。【傳播手法】以「預言」一詞定性，省略書中情節與現實事件的具體差異對照，僅憑題材相似營造先見之明的印象，屬於以文學作品回推現實的類比話術。【背景】雅各布森確為調查記者，該書屬於虛構情境作品；官方通報與媒體報導對死者診斷、是否發生實驗室事故仍有出入，書中情節與現實的對應關係無依據、未證實。

  • 2026年：安妮·雅各布森出版《Biological War: A Scenario》
  • 2026年10月：伊爾庫茨克反鼠疫研究所工作人員死亡、接觸者隔離的消息傳出
  • 2026年10月：Robin Monotti 發帖，稱該書情節與現實事件吻合、具有預言性

帖文称普京对盖茨发逮捕令，指称其在乌克兰实验室制造肺鼠疫

【说法】@Rusia_HD 发帖称，俄罗斯总统普京已对“生物学家”比尔·盖茨发出逮捕令，并称普京公布的一份 3000 页…

查看详细摘要

【说法】@Rusia_HD 发帖称，俄罗斯总统普京已对“生物学家”比尔·盖茨发出逮捕令，并称普京公布的一份 3000 页档案证明，盖茨利用人工智能工具在乌克兰生物实验室制造了肺鼠疫。【传播手法】帖文以突发新闻口吻包装未经证实的指控，用“3000 页档案”“证明”等具体化措辞增强可信感，把盖茨、普京、乌克兰实验室、人工智能等热点符号拼接成一条完整阴谋链条。【背景】所谓逮捕令与 3000 页档案均无依据、未证实；公开渠道没有普京就此发出逮捕令的记录，盖茨在乌克兰实验室用人工智能制造肺鼠疫的说法也没有任何证据支撑。【时间线】2026-10-17，伊尔库茨克疑似肺鼠疫病例消息在社交平台流传；2026-10-25，@Rusia_HD 发布该帖。

🕐 事件时间线
  • 2026-10-17：伊尔库茨克疑似肺鼠疫病例消息在社交平台流传
  • 2026-10-25：@Rusia_HD 发帖称普京对盖茨发逮捕令并指称其制造肺鼠疫
